24a2(1): shind tu mos pas dge ba'i rtsa ba myi zad payin /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi las la brtson bas /dge ba'i rtsa ba mi zad pa yin /dus gsum rab tu rnam par 'byed pas /dge ba'i rtsa ba spyi zad pa yin te /
24a3(1): de ni sems can gyi khams thams cad la /sems can myed par rab tu rtog cing yongs su bsngo'o //chos thams cad la srog myed par khong du chud cing /yongs su bsngo'o /chos thams cad la byed pa myed par yang dag 
24a4(1): pa ji lta ba bzhin du shes shing yongs su bsngo'o /dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad la gang zag myed par sems shing yongs su bsngo'o /chos thams cad nyon mongs pa myed cing /rab tu rtog cing yongs su  bsngo'o /chos thams cad
24a5(1): rtend pa yang dag par mthong zhing yongs su bsngo'o /dngos po thams cad la myi gnas pa yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du shes  shing yongs su bsngo'o /zhing thams cad la yang dag par myi gnas pa nyid du shes shing yongs 
24a6(1): su bsngo'o /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la myi gnas pa nyid du lta zhing yongs su bsngo'o /dmigs pa thams cad la dmigs su myed pa nyid du shes pas mthong zhing yongs su bsngo' //de de ltar 
24a7(1): yongs su bsngo bas zhing gang yang /kun nas nyon mongs pa dang bcas par dmyig lam du snang bar myi 'gyur ro //kun tu lta yang sems can gang yang myi 'thun ba mthong bar myi 'gyur ro /spyod pa'i tshe chos
24a8(1): gang yang yang dag par myi mthong ngo /ye shes kyi 'jug pas gang la yang 'jug pa myed do /nam mkha'i dbyings thams cad la yang rab tu gnas te /de yang shind tu shes so /bsod nams kyi stobs tshad myed pas de bzhin 
24a9(1): gshegs pa'i skus chos thams cad gang bar shind tu shes so /sems can thams cad tshim bar bya ba'i phyir /dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad du 'gro ba la yang dag par spyod do /de'i sems bskyed pa re re zhing yang sems can /
24a10(1): brjod du myed pa'i yang brjod du myed pa stobs bcu'i sar yang dag par 'grub bo //des sems can thams cad bsod nams thams cad kyis yongs su bzung zhing /thob par bya ba'i phyir dge ba'i rtsa ba rnam par dag pa bsod nams 
24a11(1): thams cad kyi 'byung gnas dang /ldan ba thams cad rab tu sgrub bo /de yo byad ci 'byad ci 'dod pa phun sum tshogs pas bsod nams kyi mdzod yid bzhin gyi nor bu lta bu dang ldan ba yin no //de gang dang gang tu 'gro ba de dang 
24b1(1): de dag tu /zhing thams cad rnam par spong zhing 'gro'o /de gang dang gang du spyod pa de dang de dag tu sems can brjod du myed pa'i yang brjod du myed pa rnam par dag par bya ba'i phyir /bsod nams kyi stobs kyis yongs su bzung ba la rab 
24b2(1): tu gzhog cing /yang dag par spyod do //de ltar yongs su bsngo ba de la rnam pa de lta bu'i rgyu 'thun bas /byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa na /gzugs myed pa yin /'jig rten thams cad kyis zil kyis 
24b3(1): myi nond pa'i bsod nams can yin /bdud thams cad kyis gzi brjid myi zad pa yin /phyir rgol ba thams cad kyis /dge ba'i rtsa ba myi 'phrogs pa yin /thams cad mkhyend pa'i yid yang dag par rtog pa dang ldan 
24b4(1): bas smon lam shind tu che ba dang /sems rab tu yangs pa yin /sems bskyed pa gcig gis sangs rgyas kyi zhing tshad myed par 'gro ba yin /ye shes kyi stobs mtha' yas pas /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yul yongs su
24b5(1): shes pa yin /sangs rgyas thams cad la shind tu dad pa yin /ye shes mtha' yas par shind tu 'jug pa la rab tu gnas pa yin /nam mkha'i dbyings kyis klas pa'i /byang chub kyi sems mngon 
24b6(1): bar bsgrubs pa'i mthus //nam mkha'i dbyings kyis klasapar chos kyi dbyings shind tu yangs pa yin no //kye rgyal pa'i sras dag 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi yongs 
24b7(1): su bsngo ba lnga pa /bsod nams myi zad pa'i mdzod ces bya ba ste /de la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni myi zad pa'i mdzod bcu rab tu 'thob bo //bcu gang zhe na /skra'i sbubs gcig tu sangs
24b8(1): rgyas grangs myed pa 'byung ba la 'jug pa'i phyir /sangs rgyas blta ba'i gter myi zad pa'i mdzod chos thams cad gcig tu rab tu gzhol ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs dang /ye shes kyi phyir chos la 'jug pa'i gter myi zad pa'i 
24b9(1): mdzod rab tu 'thob bo //de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis chos bstand pa yongs su bzung zhing chud myi zos par bya ba'i phyir /dran bas bzung ba'i gter myi zad pa'i mdzad rab tu 'thob bo //de bzhin gshegs pa 
24b10(1): thams cad kyi chos kyi tshul dang /dgongs pa shind tu bstand pa la mkhas par bya ba'i phyir /blo gros rnam par nges pa'i gter myi zad pa'i mdzod/rab tu 'thob bo //chos kyi tshul rnam par dbye ba la mkhas par bya ba'i phyir
24b11(1): rig pas 'dzind pa'i gter myi zad pa'i mjod rab tu 'thob bo /nam mkha'i dbyings dang mnyam ba'i blos dus gsum thams cad khyab par bya ba'i phyir /blo tshad myed pa'i gter myi zad pa'i brjod rab tu 'thob bo /sems 
25a1(1): //can thams cad tshim bar bya ba'i phyir /bsod nams kyi gter mi zad pa'i mdzod rab tu 'thob bo /sems can thams cad kyi mun pa dang /rab rib dang /ling tog dang /gti mug dang /ma rig pa'i mund 
25a2(1): pa rnam par bsal ba'i phyir ye shes dang /dga' ba dang /blo'i gter mi zad pa'i mdzod rab tu 'thob bo /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi chos nyid tha myi dad pa' rab tu bstand pas /sems can thams cad shes par bya ba'i phyir 
25a3(1): spobs pa rnam par nges pa'i gter rab tu 'thob bo /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa rgyun myi chad pas /thams cad mkhyend pa nyid chags pa myed pa'i thabs skyon myed pa'i dbang bskur ba'i phyir /stobs bcu dang /myi 'jigs pI
25a4(1): gter mi zad pa rab tu 'thob ste /gter mi zad pa'i mdzod bcu po 'di dag rab tu 'thob bo //de nas byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan kyis sangs rgyas kyi byind kyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te /de'i tshe 
25a5(1): tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so ///bsam bas mthu stobs rnyed pa de dag ni //mtha' yas chos la kund tu dbang bsgyurd cing //bsod nams gter la rjes su yi rang ba 
25a6(1): de dag chags myed tshul gyis yongs su bsngo //dus gsum rgyal ba ba su dag ji snyed pas //rnam dag zhing rnams 'jig rten rnam sbyang sa pas //yon tan kun dang ldan zhing 'phags pa'i rnams //
25a7(1): de 'dra'i rnam dag zhing du yongs su bsngo //dus gsum rgyal ba ji snyed bzhugs pa'i //tshul rnams kun na rjes su sems byed cing //thams cad lus pa myed par des bzung ste //de 'dra'i zhing ni 
25a8(1): brtan pa rnams kyis brgyand //dus gsum bskald pa ji snyed yod pa'i //zhing gcig yon tan de dag blang byas kyang //bskald pa ji snyed de dag zad 'gyur gyi //zhing gi yon tan bsngags pa zad myi 'gyur //'di 'dra'i zhing 
25a9(1): rnams thams cad ma lus par //myi'i mchog gis ji snyed mthong gyurd pa //thams cad ma lus zhing gi yon tan gyis //ji snyed 'jig rten de dag brgyand par snang //de dag kun kyang rnam dag yid dang ldan //de dag rgyal 
25a10(1): ba'i gsung skyes sras yin te //yon tan kun kyis brgyand pa'i sems dang ldan //zhing gi phyogs rnams rab tu rgyas par byed //de dag lus ni mtshand kyis shind tu brgyand pa//de dag dpe byad bzang pos lus kyang 
25a11(1): mdzes //de dag mtha' yas yang dag so so shes //'gro ba'i rgya mtsho myi zad ci bzhin no //de dag spyod yul dag dang yang dag ldan //ting 'dzind kun la gnas pa rjes su 'thob //mtshungs myed shind tu rnam par 
25b1(1): dag pa'i yid //thams cad phyogs rnamasu ni 'od kyang 'gyed /'di 'dra'i zhing rnams ma lus gzung byas te //nyan thos spyod yul dag la dad myed pa //rang rgyal thams cad shind tu spangs gyurd te //myi'i dam pas shind tu gang gyurd cig 
25b2(1): de dag de 'dra'i yongs bsngo sems ldan ba //gang gis ma lus 'jig rten byang chub du //dus gsum rgyal bas mngon bar sangs rgyas pa //de dang ldan bar yongs su bsngo bar byed //phyogs bcu kun na ji snyed yod pa'i //bdud dang nyon 
25b3(1): mongs de dag rab tu 'jom //dpal ldan mthu can gzhan gyis 'pham myi nus //rnam par nges par chos kyi dam pa spyod //de dag smon lam mthu stobs yangs pa yis //yongs su bsngo ba chags pa myed gyurd cing //bsod nams 
25b4(1): zad myed de dag mdzod tu zhugs //de ni dus gsum kun tu zad myi 'gyur //de dag spyod pas chos la 'jal bar byed //de dag byed pa myed pa'i chos la zhugs //g.yo myed chos kyang de dag rab tu shes //las kyi rnam pas 
25b5(1): yongs su myi sems so //gzugs ni ma yin 'ond kyang gzugs myed myin //'du shes yod myin 'du shes myed pa myin //dngos yod ma yin dngos po myed pa myin //dngos myed thams cad rang bzhin 
25b6(1): khong du chud //yod pa ma yin myed pa 'ang ma yin te //rgyu'i rgyu da la mngon bar chags myi byed //rkyen la 'ang 'jug par de dag myi byed de //nam yang de dag chags par myi byed do //'gro ba 'di 
25b7(1): na smra tshul ji snyed pa //de la nam yang chags par myi byed de //'du shes bsam ba brda yang rab tu shes //bdag myed chos kyang so sor shind tu rtogs //de dag sems can myi 'bral cis 'gyur ba 
25b8(1): de 'dra'i chos nyid mnyam bar rab tu rtogs //de 'dra'i zhing dang de bzhin las kyi rnams //dus gsum thams cad ma lus bzung byas shing //shes pas de dag de bzhin yongs su bsngo //las kyi rnam pa gang 
25b9(1): gis skye ldan zhing //rang bzhin gang dang mtshan nyid gang yin dang //de na ci 'dra'i chos nyid yod pa dag //de bzhin de dag dag par yongs su bsngo //gang gi yid kyis ngo bo nyid 'jal ba //rang bzhin byas
25b10(1): pa myed par de dag shes //'gro la de dag myi gnas lend pa myed //'gro ba kun phyir yongs su bsngo bar gzhol //spyod yul ma lus thams cad bzung byas shing //'od gsal thams cad rjes su 
25b11(1): sems par byed //ma lus de bzhin nyid kyang de dag shes //gang gis lta ba thams cad ma lus shing //bsam ba'i skyin por rnam par spangs pa'i rnams //rtag tu de dag dri myed kun gdung myed
26a1(1): /chags myed kun kyi tshul yang rjes su rtogs //de dag gis ni chos rnams ma bshig ste //nams kyang chos nyid zhig par 'gyur ba myed //chos la chags par de dag myi byed de //de dag 
26a2(1): 'jig rten sgra brnyan 'dra ba yin //gang gis dus gsum 'jig rten skyes pa yi //'gro ba kun 'byung chos kyang rab tu shes //bsam ba dag dang bag chags khong du chud //thams cad 'jig par byed pa gang yang 
26a3(1): myed //de dag las dang las myin rab tu shes //las dang rnam par 'gal bar myi byed de //las kyi rnam par smyind pa chud myi gsond //rgyu las byung ba'i chos kyang shind tu stond //'di na sems can gang yang 
26a4(1): 'khor ba myed //sems can 'byung ba de dag myi byed de //sems can rnams la gnas pa myed par smra //sems can rgyud kyi brda yang rab tu rtogs //bsod nams myi zad pa'i mdzod ces bya ba ste /yongs su 
26a5(1): bsngo ba lnga pa'o ///kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi yongs su bsngo ba drug pa dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad dang mnyam pa nyid kyi rjes su song ba zhes bya ba gang zhe na /
26a6(1): kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di la byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po rgyal por gyurd te /srid chen po thob nas /'tshe ba dang phyir rgol ba bcom ste /rgyal po chen por grags pa dang /srid chen po'i
26a7(1): khams la gnas shing /srid yangs pa la dbang bar gyurd nas /'khon myed cing ltod pa myed par srid la gnas pa /chos kyi rgyal srid kyi sa skyong bar byed pa /chos dang 'thun ba'i chos kyi rjes su snyegs pa 
26a8(1): phun sum tshogs pa'i srid chen po thob pa /sa chen po la gdugs gcig tu gyurd pa /'khor lo thogs pa myed pas skyong ba /dri ma myed pa'i thabs thob pa /srid la pha rold kyi sdes gtse ba myed pa /chos dang ldan ba dbyig 
26a9(1): pa myed pa dang mtshon cha myed pas 'gro ba rnams shind tu skyong ba /chos kyi rjes su zhugs shing /bsdu ba'i dngos po bzhis 'gro ba sdud pa /'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal por gyurd pa /'gro ba thams cad la mdzod kyis shind 
26a10(1): tu stobs pa /de dbang phyug de lta bu la gnas shing /de lta bu'i chos la gnas pa /g.yog dang 'khor mang po shind tu 'dus pa /gzugs dang ldan ba 'gro ba thams cad lta bas myi ngoms pa /yid la kha na ma tho ba myed pa /
26a11(1): bsod nams rnam par dag pa dang ldan ba /thild kyis ma nond pa'i mtshan dang ldan ba /lus shind tu rnam par phye ba /mdzes pa dang ldan ba /blta na bzang ba /tshul dang 'thun ba'i lus dang ldan ba /stobs dang ldan 
26b1(1): ba /thub pa myed pa'i lus dang ldan ba /stobs chen po dang ldan ba /mthu bo che'i lus kyis bzung ba /rnam par dag pa'i las dang ldan ba /bsgribs pa myed pa'i las rnam par dag pa dang ldan ba /de zas yongs 
26b2(1): su gtong ba 'am /btung ba 'am /ro 'am /bzhon ba 'am /gos sam /mye tog gam /'phreng ba 'am /spos sam /byug pa 'am /mal cha 'am /gnas sam /rgyab rten nam /mar mye 'am /
26b3(1): sgron ma 'am /nad kyi rkyend sman dang /yo byad rnams sbyin ba 'am /rin po ches gzhal ba'i snod sna tshogs bkang zhing yongs su gtong ba 'am /shing rta sna tshogs dang /shing rta du ma bkang zhing tshad myed pa'i rin po che dang /
26b4(1): nor yongs su gtong ba dang /glang po cang shes sam /rta cang shes rgyan thams cad kyis brgyand pa gtong ba dang /slong ba rnams la ci rigs su rgyal po'i stan bzang po gtong ba 'am /gdugs gtong ba 'am /rgyal mtshan dang 
26b5(1): ba dan gtong ba 'am /rin po che gtong zhing sbyin ba 'am /rgyan dang lhab lhub kyis sbyin ba yongs su gtong ba 'am /cod pan nam /gtsug gi nor bu dag dbang phyug la spyi bo nas /dbang bskur bas mtshon chas
26b6(1): bcings shing /btson ra na 'dug pa'i sems can rnams mthong nas /nor thams cad yongs su gtong ba dang /g.yog dang bdag tu bcas te byin nas /btson ra de dag nas sems can rnams
26b7(1): yongs su thard par byed pa /bsad par khrid pa mthong nas /bdag gi lus dang srog yongs su btang ste gsad pa de blu bar byed pa gtsug phud dang thor pag tu bcas pa'i mgo bo dag kyang slong bas /bslangs na gtong 
26b8(1): ba myed pa'i sems kyis gtong ba /myig gnyis yongs su gtong ba 'am /rna ba dang sna yongs su gtong ba 'am so dang lce yongs su gtong ba 'am mgo yongs su gtong ba 'am rka lag yongs su gtong ba 'am
26b9(1): rang gi lus kyang drald cing /slong ba la khrag yongs su gtong ba 'am sha dang rkang yongs su gtong ba 'am slong ba la rang gi lus las snying phyung ste /yongs su gtong ba 'am /slong zhing lend pa sna tshogs la rgyu ma 
26b10(1): dang /mcher pa dang /mkhal ma dang /pho ba rnams yongs su gtong ba 'am/rang gi lus las yan lag dang nying lag dang /rus pa bslangs nas dad pa'i sems kyis yongs su gtong ba 'am /slong ba la mos par lta bas 
26b11(1): pags pa dang /mdog yongs su gtong ba 'am/rka lag las sngo mo yongs su gtong ba 'am chos 'dod pa'i phyir /rang gi sha dang sen mo yongs su gtong ba 'amchos shind tu dkon ba tshol zhing myes gang ba'i 'obs
27a1(1): /myi 'greng lag tag bdun du mchong bar byed pa 'am /chos bsrung ba'i phyir /sdug bsngal myong ba thams cad kyang rang gi lus kyis bzod par byed pa 'am /chos tshol zhing chos kyi tshig dang /yi ge 'bru 
27a2(1): gcig gi phyir yang rgya mtsho'i mthar thug pa'i sa chen po yongs su gtong ba 'am /chos thams cad kyi dbang phyug byed pa 'am /myi dge ba'i chos las sems can rnams bzlog ste /dge ba'i chos la rab tu sbyor ba 'am /skyes bu'i 
27a3(1): mtshan gcod pa mthong nas /sdig pa'i las de dag las sems can de dag rnam par bzlog par byed pa 'am /de bzhin gshegs pa mngon bar sangs rgyas par 'jig rten thams cad du sgrog cing /gtsug lag khang dang pho brang gi longs 
27a4(1): spyod kyi phyir sa dang /khams yongs su gtong ba 'am rim gro bya ba'i phyir bran dang las byed pa'i myi rnams gtong ba 'am /slong ba la rim gro bya ba'i phyir /bdag nyid 'bul ba 'am /chos la dad pas dag zhing mgu ba'i sems rnyed 
27a5(1): nas /rang gi lus kyis de bzhin gshegs pa la rim gro byed pa 'am /bdag nyid sems can thams cad la gtong ba 'am /sangs rgyas thams cad la rim gro bya ba'i phyir /rgyal srid yongs su gtong ba 'am /slong 
27a6(1): ba rnams la ci rigs su yul dang /myir bcas pa 'am /grong khyer ra dang /rgyal po'i pho brang ngam /rgyan thams cad kyis brgyand pa yongs su gtong ba 'am /slong ba la bu dang bu mo dang chung ma dag yongs su 
27a7(1): gtong ba 'am /slong ba rnams la ci rigs su khyim thams cad yongs su sbyin zhing /gtong ba 'am /bza' shing gi ra ba sbyin ba 'am /slong ba sna tshogs dang /lend pa sna tshogs dang /sems can sna tshogs dang 
27a8(1): gang zag sna tshogs sems can sna tshogs dang ldan pa la /phyogs tha dad pa nas 'ongs pa /tshad las 'das pa'i myi dang /myi sna tshogs dang /sbyin ba'i gnas sna tshogs la /mchod sbyin bye ba khrag khrig brgya phrag stong du ma grangs myed pa 
27a9(1): rnams byed cing /yongs su gtong ba 'am /de sbyin ba dmyigs pa'i /dngos po thams cad yongs su gtong ba'i tshe /sems 'di ltar bskyed de /yongs su bsngo zhing /dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad kyi snying po'i rjes su song bas /
27a10(1): gzugs shind tu bzung ba /dge ba'i rtsa ba thams chad kyi snying po'i rjes su songs bas /tshor ba shind tu bzung ba /dgeba'i rtsa ba thams cad kyi snyid po'i rjes su song bas /'du shes shind tu bzung ba /dge ba'i rtsa ba thams can kyi snying po'i 
27a11(1): rjes su song bas /'du byed shind tu bzung ba /dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad gyi snying po'i rjes su song ba sa /rnam par she pa shind tu bzung ba //dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad kyi snying po'i rjes su song bas /srid thams cad shind tu bzung
27b1(1): ba /dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad kyi snying po'i rjes su song bas /'khor shind tu bzung ba /dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad kyi snying po'i rjes su song bas /longs spyod shind tu bzung ba /dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad kyi snying po'i rjes su song bas/sbyin ba
27b2(1): shind tu bzung ba de /dge ba'i rtsa ba de dag 'di ltar yongs su bsngo ste /bla na myed par dmyigs nas /bza' ba dang btung ba 'di dag 'di ltar btang ba dang /'di ltar byin ba dang /'di ltar chags pa myed pa dang /'di ltar bcings pa 
27b3(1): myed pa dang /'di ltar nye bar btang ba dang /'di lta ma bzung ba dang /'di ltar lhag par bsam ba dang /'di ltar dngos po yongs su btang ba dang /'di ltar dmyigs pa la myi gnas pa dang /'di ltar gtong ba dang ldan bas /
27b4(1): sems can thams cad ye shes kyi kha zas dang /ldan ba dang /chags pa myed pa la brtson ba sems kyi zas yongs su shes pa dang /chags pa myed pa'i zas dang /dga' ba'i zas dang /zang zing myed pa'i zas dang /ye shes kyis
27b5(1): 'tsho ba dang /chos kyis brtan ba dang /dge ba'i rtsa bas yongs su bzung ba'i chos kyi sku dang /ye shes kyi sku la yang dag par spyod pa dang /sbyin ba'i gnas dang /snying brtse ba'i kha zas rab tu lend par gyurd
27b6(1): cig ces /de ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po za sa sbyin ba de dag yongs su bsngo'o //byang chub sems dpa' btung ba'i sbyin ba gtong ba'i tshe /'di ltar yongs su bsngo ste /
27b7(1): dge ba'i rtsa ba 'dis sems can thams cad /chos kyi btung ba la brtson ba dang /byang chub sems dpa'i lam dang ldan ba dang /'jig rten pa'i skom ba rgyun chad pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kyis
27b8(1): skom ba'i las myi ldog pa dang /'dod pa'i yul gyis skom ba las phyir ldog pa dang /chos kyis tshim ba'i dga' ba rab tu thob pa dang /chos kyi sku shind tu tshim ba dang /ting nge 'dzind kyis tshim bar byed pa'i sems dang ldan ba dang /

157a11(1): tshad myed pa /brjod du myed pa'i yang brjos du myed pa /yongs su bstand pas sa 'tshams su 'gyur ba myed do //de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di lta ste dper na /mye ni ji srid du shing yod pa de snyed du 'bar te /mye 'bar 
157b1(1): ba la mtha' thug pa myed do //de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang ci tsam du /sems can gyi khams la /rnam par dpyod pa dang /ci tsam du chos kyi dbyings la 'jug pa dang /ci tsam du 'jig rten gyi khams su rgyu ba dang /
157b2(1): ci tsam du nam mkha'i rang bzhin gyi tshad myi dmyigs pa dang /ci tsam du sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du myed pa'i yang /brjod du myed pa'i rdul shind tu phra ba snyed kyi /sangs rgyas kyi zhing /gcig nas brgyud pa'i sems kyi /skad cig re re la 
157b3(1): 'jug cing /khong du chud par bya ba dang /ci tsam du thams cad mkhyend pa'i ye shes dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'byam klas pa /brjod du myed pa'i yang brjod du myed pa dag gi chos kyi bye brag rnam par phye ba la 'jug pa dang /ci
157b4(1): tsam du sems can gyi khams gyi rgyud /brjod du myed pa'i yar brjod du myed pa /dge ba'i rtsa ba la gzhog cing nye bar 'tsho bar byed pa dang /nye bar rtond pa dang /shind tu rnam par dag par byed pa dang /ci tsam du byang chub sems 
157b5(1): dpa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba dam pa brjod du myed pa'i yang /brjod du myed pa yang dag par mthong zhing /smon lam gcig tu nges pa myed pa kund tu bzang po 'i spyod pa dang /smon lam la nges par bya ba dang /kund tu bzang po'i 
157b6(1): spyod yul gyi ye shes kyi sgo la gzhog pa dang /ci tsam du dus gsum la 'jug pa'i rgyud brjod du myed pa'i yang /brjod du myed pa 'byam klas pa dang /snyoms shing rnam par rtog pa'i dus la 'jug pas /mtha' dang 
157b7(1): dbung myed pa'i tshul du 'jug pa dang /ci tsam du'ajig rten gyi khams 'abyung ba dang /'jig pa dang /gnas pa brjod du myed pa'i yar brjod du myed pa /rnam par rtog pa'i dmyigs pa kund tu rgyu ba 'byung zhing 
157b8(1): rgyun myi 'chad pa de tsam du /ye shes kyi dmyigs pa snying rje chen po las byung ba /smon lam chen pos spruld pa /'gro ba thams cad ma lus pa /gdul ba la brtson ba yang mthar thug ces bya ba myed do //de ci'i 
157b9(1): phyir zhe na /'di ltar de ni byang chub sems dpa' kund tu bzang po'i spyod pa dang /smon lam yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i phyir /byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i /spyod pa'i khyad par ye shes kyis 
157b10(1): bsgrubs pas /'gro ba thams cad yongs su dgrol bar bya ba la /brtson ba'i phyir ro //de bas na byang chub sems dpas /ting nge 'dzind kyi rnam pa 'di lta bu dang /de lta bu'i yul de ltar 
157b11(1): rgya che ba dang /de ltar yangs pa dang /de ltar tshad myed pa dang /de ltar bsam gyis myi khyab pa dang /de ltar 'od kun tu mtha' yas pa dang /de ltar sangs rgyas thams cad mngon tu gyurd pa dang /de 
159a1(1): //to /de bzhin du chos kyi dbyings mthas klas pa'i phyogs bcu ma lus par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad nas kyang de bzhin gshegs pa kun kyis zhal mngon bar bstand te /byang chub sems dpa' de ye shes chen po'i ting nge 'dzind dang
159a2(1): snyoms par 'jug pa kun tu bzang po'i spyod yul la yang dag par gnas pa'i rnam pa de lta bu dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul la rmongs pa myed cing chos thams cad la rab tu gzhol bar bya ba'i phyir /de bzhin gshegs pa'i blo gros nye bar sgrub pa mdzad do /
159a3(1): thams cad mkhyend pa nyid du yang dag par gzhol zhing chos rnam par tha dad pa thams cad kyi don khong du chud par bya ba'i phyir blo gros nye bar sgrub pa ma mdzad do //chags pa la rtogs par gzhol bas sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad yongs su 'dzind par bya ba'i phyir rig
159a4(1): pa nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad do //ye shes 'byam klas pas khong du chud par bya ba dang /blos rnam par sangs rgyas pa'i chos kyi dbyings kyi yul gyi mthas klas pa thams cad la sangs rgyas kyi byang chub nye bar sgrub pa mdzad do//byang chub sems dpa' de chos
159a5(1): kyi snang ba dang /'od thams cad kyis mun pa myed pa'i ye shes 'byam klas pa las phyir myi ldog par bya ba'i phyir shes rab nye bar sgrub pa mdzad do  //byang chub sems dpa' 'di thams mkhas pas 'jig rten thams cad las 'phags pas sems can
159a6(1): 'dul ba la bsgribs pa myed par bya ba'i phyir ye shes nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad //byang chub sems dpa'i de chos kyi rgyud mtha' yas pa brjod pa la zad pa shes pa'i tha dad pa yang dag par shes pa bskyed pa'i phyir spobs pa nye bar
159a7(1): bsgrub pa mdzad do //byang chub sems dpa'ade 'gro ba thams cad du lus brjod du myed pa'i yang brjod du myed pa'i rgyud dang /kha dog mtha' yas pa rnam pa tha dad pa rnam par stond par bya ba 'di la nye bar 'a
159a8(1): sbug pa mdzad do //byang chubasems dpa' de sems can sna tshogs thams cad la dbyangs kyi yan lag dang ldan ba'i sgra dbyangs dang skad sna tshogs stond par bya ba'i phyir lce yangs pa nye bar sgrub pa mdzad do//byang chub sems dpa'de mngon
159a9(1): thag tu stond pa dang thos par byed pas sems can thams cad yongs su smyind par bya ba la rmongs pa myed par bya ba'i phyi rmongs pa myed pa nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad do //des byang chub sems dpa' de byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa yongs
159a10(1): su grub pa yin //yongs su grub nas kyang thams cad mkhyend pa'i sgo'i yongs su rdzogs par bya bas de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs la 'jug pa'o //'byung ba'i sgo yongs su dag par byed do //chags pa myed pa'i sgos yongs su 'byung bar
159a11(1): byed do //sems can thams cad 'byung ba'i sgo yongs su rtsom zhing byind kyis rlob bo //chos thams cad kyi rnam par 'phrul pa'i sgo yang dag par stond to //cho 'phrul gyi sgo tshad myed pas sems can 'dul ba la rab tu 'jug go //
159b1(1): byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa'i rnam pa la rab tu mkhas par sbyong ngo //sangs rgyas thams cad kyi khyu mchog gi mthu' phyung ba la byang chub sems dpa' kunatu bzang po'i spyod pas yang dag par 'byung ngo //phyi ma'i mtha'i mung thug
159b2(1): pa'i 'gro ba thams cad gdul ba'i phyir sangs rgyas kyi chos chos thams cad rnam par phye ba'i chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor ba la 'gro ba thams cad 'dul ba'i mthar thug pa byind kyis rlabs kyis byin du rlob bo //de ci'i phyir zhe na /byang chub sems dpa' ni smon lam
159b3(1): gyi khyad par de lta bu dang ldan pas yongs su grub bam /grub na 'jig rten thams cad kyi slob dpon du 'gyur ba yin /chos kyi nyi ma'i 'jig rten du shard pa yin /ye shes kyi bzla' ba 'jig rten thams cad snang bar 'byung ba yin /ye shes dam pa'i ri rab myi'
159b4(1): g.yo ba 'jig rten thams cad las mngon par 'phags pa yin /ye shes kyi rgya mtsho 'tshad myed pa 'jig rten thams cad du yang dag par gnas pa yin /bsgrub pa rgyun myi 'chad pas 'jig rten thams cad du chos kyi nan tan byed pa yin /'jig rten gyi khams sa
159b5(1): 'tshams myed pa thams cad du rab tu snang ba yin /rnam par dag pa yangs shing mtha' yas pa 'gro ba thams cad du brjod pa yin /sems can gyi khams mtha' dang dbung myed pa dge ba'i rtsa ba dang bsod nams kyi rgyud thams cad mkhyend pa nyid
159b6(1): dang ldan pa'i smon lam chen po la rab tu brtson par gzhog pa yin /kun cing rab tu sbyor ro //de ltar byang chub sems dpa' de de lta bu'i ngang tshul can de lta bu'i ye shes can /de lta bu'i chos thob par gyurd pa de lta bu'i
159b7(1): myed pa'i yang brjod du myed pa la sems can gyi khams yang dag par gzhog //chos de lta bu mthong bar gyurd pa mngon par shes pa de lta bu'i yid dang ldan pa /cho 'phrul dam pa de lta bu dang ldan pa /cho 'phrul mchog tu bya ba phul du bya ba de lta bus yang dag par grub
159b8(1): pa rtag tu brtson pa'i snying rje chen pos mngon par 'phags pa chos kyi don bzang po la rtag tu rgyun myi 'chad par brtson pa /bde' ba'i lam yang dag par stond pa /bsod nams chen po dang ye shes yangs pa'i 'od gzer gyi rgyal mtshan gyurd pa rnam par
159b9(1): thard pa bsam gyis myi khyab pa shind tu thob bo //thams cad mkhyend pa'i spyod yul la don yod par rjes su song ba /sangs rgyas kyi rnam par thard pa'i pha rold tu phyind pa thob pa /rnam par thard pa'i tshul shin tu phra ba bsam gyis myi khyab pa rab tu
159b10(1): bsgoms pa chos kyi dbyings kyi rgyud dang /tshul rmongs pa myed par khong du chud pa /kun tu bzang po'i ting nge 'dzind brjod du myed pa la rnam par rtse zhing spyod pa dang /seng ge bsgyings pa'i ye shes kyis grub pa la shin tu gnas pa /sems dang yid
160a1(1): /dang rnam par shes pa la chags pa myed pa rnams kyi chos kyi bsam pa dang /lhag pa'i bsam pa rab tu yangs pa chen po bcu 'byung ngo//bcu gang zhe na 'di la byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas thams cad rjes su dran pas gnas pa yin
160a2(1): chos thams cad kyi de bzhin nyid tshad myed pas gnas pa yin/sems can thams cad 'dul ba'i snying rje chen pos gnas pa yin/zhing rnam par dag pa'i gnas bsam gyis myi khyab pa'i phyir gnas rnam par dag pas gnas pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom

160a4(1): kyi ngo bo nyid dang /rang bzhin mtshan nyid myed pas gnas pa yin /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dge' ba'i rtsa ba 'das pa dang ma byond pa la/da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas/thams cad dang rgyud kyi tshul gcig tu gyurd pas gnas pa yin/de bzhin

160a11(1): 'byung bar bya ba'i phyir byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa thams cad yongs su grub pa yang rab tu 'thob bo //sangs rgyas thams cad kyi chos kyi gzungs kyis 'das pa dang /ma byond pa da ltar 'byung ba'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyi bstand pa yang dag par 'dzind
160b1(1): to //'dod chags dang bral ba'i mtha'ar sems can gyi khams thams cad la yang dag par blta'o //tshig gi lam gyi rgyud kyis sems can thams cad 'dul ba yongs su stond to //de ci'i phyir zhe na /ting nge 'dzind rgya chen po de lta bu thob pa'i
160b2(1): byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi chos nyid de //des byang chub sems dpa' de sangs rgyas kyi ye shes 'byam klas pa thams cad la mchog tu bya ba rab tu 'thob bo //de'i phyir chos 'byam klas pa thams can stond pa la mkhas pa'i tha dad pa yang dag par
160b3(1): shes pa rab tu 'thob bo //de'i phyir 'jig rten thams cad du chos kyi dam pa 'jigs pa myed pa rnam par dag pa rab tu 'thob bo /de'i phyir ting nge 'dzind thams cad kyi rjes su 'gro ba'i ye shes rab tu 'thob bo //de 'i phyir byang chub sems dpa'i tshul
160b4(1): thams cad la mkhas pa'i dam pa rab tu 'thob bo //de'i'i phyir tshul thams cad kyi rgyan rab tu 'thob bo //de'iphyir 'jig rten thams cad ngu chos kyis dbugs 'byind pa'i pha rold tu phyind pa dang ldan par 'gyur ro //de'i phyir sems can thams cad kyi
160b5(1): dus dang dus ma yin pa rab tu shes so //de'i phyir 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad snang bar bya ba rab tu shes so //de'i pyir sems can thams cad la bsgribs pa /myed pa'i ye shes khong du chud par 'gyur ro //de'i phyer don thams cad bsgrub
160b6(1): par yon tan thams cad la yan lag rab tu gnas par 'gyur ro //de'i  phyir theg pa'i rab tu brtson par bya ste /sems can thams cad la mnyam bar bzhag pa rnam par dag pa rab tu brjod do //de ci'i phyir zhe na /de ni byang chuba' seMs
160b7(1): dpa' sems can gyi don la gnas pa snying rje chen po de lta bu rnam par 'phel bar byed pa dge' ba'i bshes gnyen dang bstend par 'dod pa dang /sangs rgyas thaMs cad la blta bar 'dod pa /chos thams cad snang bar bya 'dod pa zhing thams
160b8(1): cad du 'gro bar 'dod pa /phyogs thams cad du rgyu bar 'dod pa /dus gsum mnyam ba nyid khong du chud par 'dod pa /chos mnyam ba nyid mngon bar 'tshang rgya bar 'dod pa /sangs rgyas mnyam ba nyid la rab tu gnas par 'dod pa /ye shes mnyam ba nyid
160b9(1): la 'dug par 'dod pas chos 'di dag la las de lta bur brtson zhing las gzhan byed pa dang /ngoms myi shes pa dang /sems can ma 'khrugs pa dang /yid rtse gcig tu gyurd pa dang /de ltar gnas pa la brtsom ba dang /de ltar khong du chud pa'i yid la 
160b10(1): de ltar ma nyams pa'i sems dang /de lta bu'i yid bde' ba dang /de ltar las su rung ba dang /de ltar brtson ba dang /de ltar mthar 'byind pa dang /de ltar rnam par spyod pas nan tan du bya zhing mngon bar bsgrub par bya'o //de ci'i phyir zhe na /byang 
160b11(1): chub sems dpa' rnams ni log par smra ba ma yin /byang chub sems dpa' rnams ni gzhan du smra ba ma yin te /byang chub sems dpa'-i rnams ni ji skad du smras pa de bzhin du byed pa'o //de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar dper na /rdo rje ni gcig tu myi rung
161a1(1): /bar brjod par ste/myi chogs pa'i rang bzhin yongs su myi gtong ngo //de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang chos 'di dag las brjod pa ste/'di lta bu'i chos la gnas pa yongs su myi gtong ngo //'di lta ste dper na gser ni kha dog
161a2(1): shin tu bzang bar brjod pa ste/gser gyi kha dog bzang po rnam par dag pa yongs su myi gtong ngo//de bzhin du byang chub kyi sems dpa' yang de ltar dge zhing bzang ba'i chos kyis brjod pa ste/de ltar dge' zhing bzang ba'i chos la gnas pa yongs gsum myi gtong ngo //'di
161a3(1): lta ste dper na lha'i bu nyi ma ni snang ba'i dkyil 'khor gyis brjod pa ste/snang ba yongs su myi gtong ngo /de bzhin du byang chub sem dpa' yang 'gro ba thams cad du snang bas brjod pa ste/byang chub sems dpa'i snang ba yongs su myi gtong //'di lta ste dper
161a4(1): na ri'i rgyal po ri rab ni rin po che sna bzhi'i phung pos rgya mtsho' las shind tu 'phags pa yongs su myi gtong //de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang 'jig rten thams cad las dge' ba'i rtsa bas shin tu 'phags par brjod pa ste/'jig rten thams cad las shin tu
161a5(1): 'phags pa yongs su myi gtog ngo //'di lta ste dper na sa chen po ni 'gro ba thams cad yongs su rton par brjod pa ste/rton pa'i rang bzhin yongs su myi gtong ngo //de bzhin du byang chub semams dpa' yang 'gro ba thams ces yongs su myi 'greld pas
161a6(1): brjod pa ste/snying rje chen po yongs su myi gtong ngo//'di lta ste dper na rgya mtsho' chen po ni rin po che thams cad kyi 'byung gnas su brjod pa ste/rgya mtsho'i chu yongs su myi gtong ngo //de bzhin du byang chub sem dpa' yang
161a7(1): yang dge' ba'i rtsa ba thams cad kyi 'byung gnasasu brjod pa ste/sems can thams cad yongs su 'grol ba'i smon lam yongs su myi gtong ngo//'di lta ste dper na daphong gi slob dpon ni mdag zhu dang 'phong la zo mdog bde' bas
161a8(1): ji srid du mda' gzhu' dang ldan zhing mda' gzhu la brtson bas brjod par bya ba ste/'phong gi tshul du bya ba yongs su gtong ngo//de bzhin du byang chub kyi sems dpa' yang ci tsam du ting nge 'dzind kyi sgo dang yul gyi rnam pa 'di lta bu la mngon bar brtson zhing yongs
161a9(1): su bsgrub pa de tsam du thams cad mkhyend pa'i ye shes kyi snang bar 'gyur ba yin/'di lta ste dper na 'khor los bsgyur ba'i rgyal po ni ci tsam du tshe ring ba de tsam du bde' ba myong ba yin/de bzhin du byang chub se mas dpag pa ji tsam du byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa
161a10(1): spyod cing rnam pa de lta bu'i ting nge chen po la snyoms par 'jug pa de tsam du sems can gyi khams rnam par dag par 'gyur ro//shind tu rnam par dag pa yang rab tu 'thob bo//'di lta ste dper na ci tsam du sa bon btab pa de tsam du rnam par 'phel lo//
161a11(1): de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang ci tsam tu byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa la brtson pa de tsam du sems kyi khams rnams dge' ba'i rtsa ba la rnam par dag par 'gyur lo//'di lta ste dper na sprin chen po ni ci tsam du char 'bebs pa de tsam du
161b1(1): dbyar gyi dus la sa bo la rnam par 'phel bar 'gyur ro //de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang ci tsam du chos kyi sprin gyi char 'ang bas shing byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa dang /rnam par 'di lta bu'i ting nge 'dzind chen po la snyoms par 'jug pa de tsam du
161b2(1): sems can rnams shind tu rnam par dag par 'gyur ro //shind tu mthar thug par 'gyur ba dang /shin tu bde' ba phun sum tshogs pa dang ldan ba dang /mchog tu bya ba shind tu 'thob pa dang /shind tu pha rold tu phyind pa dang /'jig rten thams cada'
161b3(1): shind tu tshim bar byed pa dang /skye dgu thams cad kyi bya ba thams cad la the tsom shin tu gcod pa dang /basbyin bsregs yongs su gzung zhing sbyin ba yongs su sbyong bas bsod nams rmad du 'byung ba dang /phyir myi ldog pa'i 'khor lo la rab tu gzhog pa'i phyir thaMs
161b4(1): cad mkhyend pa nyid du skal ba shin tu mnyam bar 'gyur ba dang /sems can thams cad khams gsum las bsgral ba'i phyir ye shes shin tu rnam par dag par byed pa yin /thams cad mkhyend pa'i ye shes la sems can rnams 'god pa'i phyir chos kyi kha lo
161b5(1): bsgyur ba dam par 'gyur ba yin /de ci'i phyir zhe na /byang chub sems dpa' ni rang bzhin gyis chos kyi dbyings rnam par phye ba ste bsam gyis myi khyab pa'o //tshad myed pa'o /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa rnam par sbyong ba'o /thams
161b6(1): cad mkhyend pa nyid kyi phyir ye shes sbyong ba'o /yongs su 'dul bas sems can sbyong ba'o /yongs su bsngo bas zhing sbyong ba'o /rab tu gzhog pas chos sbyong ba'o /zhum ba myed pas myi 'jigs pa sbyong ba'o /rab
161b7(1): tu bstand pas tha dad pa yang dag par shes pa sbyong ba'o /shind tu bstand pas chos sbyong ba'o /chos thams cad las phyir myi ldog par gzungs sbyong ba'o /sangs rgyas kyi 'byung ba thams cad kyi don tu sangs rgyas kyi bstan
161b8(1): pa sbyong ba'o /de ltar byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzind 'di la gnas pa ni yon tan 'di dag dang gzhan yang yongs su dag pa brjod du myed pa'i yang brjod du myed pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya' stong rab tu 'thob bo /ting nge 'dzind chen po'i dkyil 'khor
161b9(1): 'di dag gi dbang dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi byind kyi rlabs dang /rang gi dge' ba'i rtsa ba'i rgyu'i stobs dang /ye shes kyi sa'i sgrol ba'i mthu' dang /dge' ba'i bshes gnyen gyis nye bar bstand pa'i stobs dang /bdud kyi las thams

169b1(1): dang ldan ba'i kha dog dang /bsam gyis myi khyab pa'i tshul dang ldan ba'i kha dog dang/zild kyis gnond pa'i kha dog dang /thams cad zild kyis gnond pa'i khadog dang/thams cad zil du rlag par byed pa'i kha dog dang /rab rib myed pa'i kha dog 
169b2(1): dang /mu myed pa'i kha dog dang /shin tu mnyam ba'ir bzhag pa'i kha dog dang /sems dpe' ba'i kha dog dang /mtshan sna tshogs dang ldan ba kha dog dang /dpe' byad bzang po sna tshogs dang ldan ba'i kha dog dang /che ba nyid dang ldan ba'i kha dog

169b4(1): dang /gnas pa myed pa'i kha dog dang /kun mtha' yas pI kha dog dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du myed par rab tu 'dul ba'i kha dog dang /rnam par 'phel ba'i kha dog dang /snying bo bzang ba'i kha dog dang /yon tan bachog dang ldan ba'i

169b9(1): pa'i kha dog dang /don yod pa'i kha dog dang /byams pa chen pos yang dag par 'phags pa'i kha dog dang /snying rje chen pos byin kyis brlabs pa'i kha dog dang /tshul mthun bar byung ba'i kha dog dang /bsos nams kyi bstobs dang ldan ba'i
169b10(1): kha dog dang /rjes su dran bar bya ba'i kha dog dang /rin po che mtha' yas pa'i kha dog dang /rin po che'i dam pa rnam par snang ba'i kha dog dang /'gro ba thams cad dad par byed pa'i kha dog dang /thams cad mkhyend pa nyid mngon tu gyurd pa'i
169b11(1): kha dog dang /bzhin 'dzum pa'i kha dog dang /rin po cha'i rgyan dam pa thams cad dang ldan ba'i kha dog dang /sems spyi gnas pa'i kha dog dang /gnas myed pa'i kha dog dang /byind kyi brlabs rnam par 'phrul ba'i kha dog dang /rdzu 'phrul
174a1(1): //smyig rgyu ba ni yul na 'ang myi gnas /phyogs na mying myi gnas pa'o //de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'ang yul na 'ang myi gnas/phyogs na myi gnas pa'i chos nyid rab tu shes te /
174a2(1): 'jig rten nang na 'ang myi gnas /phyi rol na yang myi gnas /yod pa 'ang ma yin /myed pa yang ma yin ba dang /rtag pa 'ang ma yin chad pa ma yin bar rnam par rig par gyurd pa rab tu shes so //yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin 
174a3(1): rab tu shes so /nang zhes bya ba nang na gnas pa yang ma yin ba dang nang zhes bya ba phyi rol na gnas pa ma yin ba dang /kha dog gcig pa ma yin ba dang /kha tog tha dad pa ma yin ba yang rab tu shes te /chos thams cad kha dog myed pa'i sa thob 
174a4(1): par bya ba yang yongs su rdzogs par byed do /kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po rnams kyi smyig rgyu lta bu'i bzod pa thob pa ste lnga pa'o //kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen

174a6(1): 'byung ba ma yin /'jig rten las 'das pa yang ma yin /rmi lam ni 'dod pa'i khams kyang ma yin /gzugs kyi khams kyang ma yin //gzugs myed pa'i khams kyang ma yino //rmi lam ni shi
174a7(1): 'pho ba yang ma yin /skye ba yang ma yin no //rmi lam ni kun nas nyon mongs pa yang ma yin /rnam par byang ba yang ma yin no //myi lam ni lta ba 'ang ma yin /rnam par dag pa 'ang ma yin te/rmi lam snang

174b1(1): byed kyang 'pho ba mye //de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung ngan na brjod pa yang ma yin bar rab tu shes so //de bzhin gshegs pa'i phyi rold na rjod pa yang ma yin bar rang du shes so /
174b2(1): de ltar shes ba de yang nang nas shes pa yang ma yin bar rab du shes so //phyi rol nas shes pa yang ma yin par rab tu shes so /phyi nang nas shes pa 'ang ma yin bar rab tu shesaso //'ond kyang bstand pa mngon bar bsgrub pa 'ang rab 
174b3(1): tu shes so //byind kyi rlabs la mkhas pas 'jug pa 'ang rab tu shes so /tshig kyang rab tu shes so /sgra brnyan lta bur rten cing 'breld par 'byung bar grags par gyurd pa'i chos nyid kyang 'khrug par myi byed de /sgra 'dogs par 
174b4(1): yang myi byed la /dbyangs kyi yan lag rnam pa tha dad pa la 'jug cing bslab ba la 'ang shind tu bslab pa yin /'di lta ste dper lha'i dbang po brgya' byin gyis lha'i bud myed 'grogs ma'i kha'i dbugs kyis lan cig bus pas /
174b5(1): kha'i dbugs yan lag stong dang ldan ba 'byung ngo //kha'i dbugs sa la rnam par rtog pa myed kyang dbyangs kyi yan lag rnams stong du sgrog go //de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po yang b.ra' 
174b6(1): rtog pa dang ldan ba'i dbyings la 'jug pa'i tshe /dbyangs kyi yan lag mtha' yas pa rnam par bsgom ba la mkhas dang dbung myed par 'gro ba thams cad la phyir myi ldog pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba la rab tu 'jug 
174b7(1): cing 'khor rnams la yang rgya cher yang dag par stond to /lce shin tu yangs pa yang byind kyis rlob ste /dbyangs chags pa myed pa 'ang rab tu brjod do /zhing thams cad du 'gengs pa'i dbyangs kyang byin
174b8(1): kyis rlob bo /sems can rnams kyis mtha' yas par khong du chud par yang byin gyis rlob bo /chos rnam par phye ba 'byam klas pa yang rab tu snang bar byed do //dbyangs thams cad la 'jug pa yang rab tu shes so /
174b9(1): sgra rnam par dmyigs pa yang rnam par dmyigs pa myed pa rab tu shes so /dbyangs sna tshogs kyang rnam 'god do /dbyangs kyi yan lag gi dkyil 'khor khong du chud pas pas kyang rab tu spyod do /kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ni

175a4(1): cad kyang yongs su rdzogs par byed de /'jig rten gyi rgyud kyi rjes su 'brang ba yang ma yin no /chos kyi rgyud thogs par byed pa 'ang ma yin no /'di lta ste dper na gzugs brnyan ni nyi ma 'am zla ba 'am /bud myed dma'
175a5(1): skyes pa 'am shing ngam ri 'am /gnas sam rtsig pa 'am sa 'am chu klung gi rgyud dam /dngos po sna tshogs mtha' yas pa tshad myed pa rnams kyi gzugs gang dang gang yod pa de dag thams cad rnam par dmyigs par byed
175a6(1): byed de /chu 'am til ram ram /nor bu 'am mching bu 'am mye long ngam /gzhan yang yo byad kha dog dang ba /mdog gsal ba gang yang rung ste /de dag shind tu dang ba'i phyir de dag du
175a7(1): gzugs brnyan rnam par dmyigs par byed kyang /gzugs brnyan ni shin tu dang ba'i dbyings de dang 'dres pa yang myed de /gzugs brnyan de de las gud na 'dug pa yang ma yin no /gzugs

178a1(1): /de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po yang chos thams cad la /mngon sum du gyurd pa ste /byang chub sems dpa' ni chos thams cad kyi mngon sum du gyurd pa ma yin no //

178b4(1): thogs pa myed pa'i tshul du /dbyangs mtha' yas shing /tshad myed pa rab tu 'thob bo /namamakha' ltar thams cad la thogs pa myed pa'i tshul du/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa thams cad la mkhas pas /yongs

178b10(1): kyi bzod pa thob pa bcu'o //de nas byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po kund tu bzang pos /don 'di nyid de bas kyang /gsal bar bstand pa'i phyir /byang chub sems dpa'i de dag la tshigs su bcad de smras pa 
178b11(1): ji ltar skyes bu la la zhig //rin cen 'byung gnas thos gyurd na //nor tshogs chen po bstsags don du //rab tu dga' ba skye ba yin //de bzhin shes rab che ldan ba'i //byang chub sems dpa' rgyal sras rnams 
180a1(1): //bzod pa 'di gnas pa //de la phyogs kun sangs rgyas mtha' yas lung yang stond //rgyal sras de la sangs rgyas mkhyend pas dbang bskur mdzad //phyogs bcu kun nas rab tu snang bar mngon tu gnas /
180a2(1): de dag chos kun rab zhi rtag tu zhi ba'i sems //mngon bar sgrub cing /dge ba'i kun la 'gro ba 'god //dus gsum chos rnams kun kyi mtshan nyid de 'dra bas //mtshan rnams yongs su dag byas sems can 'dul ba byed //sgyu ma
180a3(1): lta bu'i chos rnams nges par sus rtogs pa //de dag 'jig rten sna tshogs du ma rtogs byed cing //'jig rten sna tshogs sgyu tshul yang dag rtogs byas nas //las yangs shind tu bstsags pas rnam pa sna tshogs pa //sems las
180a4(1): las kyi rnam pa du ma 'byung bar 'gyur //de bas 'jig rten sems de sgyu ma lta bur bstand //de dag rnam brtags sgyu ma 'dra bas nub par 'gyur //srid pa'i lam rgyud ma lus rgyu 'dra yang dag myin //ji ltar sgyu ma'i rnam pa byind
180a5(1): rlabs byas pa'i gzugs //lam gyi bzhi mdor 'jig rten phal gyi nang bstand pa //'gro bas mthong na de la kun kyang mos pa skyed //sgyu mas 'jig rten las ni gang yang 'grub myi 'gyur //de bzhin
180a6(1): 'gro ba ji snyed gang rnams yod pa dag //sgyu ma'i rang bzhin lta bu ma skyes chos nyid de //dngos yod dngos myed de bzhin yod dang myed pa dang //skal ba myi mnyam tshul gyi 'gro rgyud rab tu stond //sgyu ma'i
180a7(1): tshul rnams shes nas sems can yongs su 'greld //sgyu ma lta bur yin bar sems can khong du chud //sems can sgyu 'dra de bzhin sgyu ma sems can 'dra //sems can myed cing sgyu ma 'dra bar 'jig rten shes //
180a8(1): sems can zhing dang chos dang de bzhin dus gsum dang //'jig rten thams cad sgyu ma'i rnam par rab tu 'byed //sgyu ma'i rnam pa lta bur chos rnams rab rtogs pas //'jig rten ma lus 'di dag sgyu ma'i rnam par shes //skyes
180a9(1): pa dag dang de bzhin bud myed rogs po dang //bu mo de bzhin rnam par 'dul ba nye bar stond //glang po rta dang ba lang phyugs dang shing rta rnams //me tog 'bras bu bzang dang ldan ba'i mtsho yang stond //thabs kyi rnam pa
180a10(1): sna tshogs mang bar sgyu myi sems //sna tshogs gnas la 'ang sgyu ma yongs su gnas pa myed //sgyu ma dben la gzhol zhing shind tu zhi ba yang //'jig rten rnam par rtog pa sna tshogs mang po stond //de bzhin rgyal sras
180a11(1): btsun ba rigs dang ldan ba'i rnams //'jig rten sgyu ma sna tshogs ji bzhin stond par byed //bsgom dang rnam par bsgom ba'i dngos po'i sems kyang myed //dngos po dngos myed sgyu ma ji bzhin rab tu shes //zhing
180b1(1): dang sems can chos kyi rnam pa mang por bcas //las rnams sna tshogs rnam pas yongs su bstsags pa la //gnas par myi byed mngon bar chags par de bzhin myed //chags myed mtha'i sgyu ma'i tshul ni des shes so ///de ltar
180b2(1): rgyal la sras mkhas pa'i rnams //rab tu zhi la dga' zhing spros pa myed pa ste //gnas myed sa dang yang dag ldan //chos dbyings gnas la de dag rab tu zhugs //de ni chos nyid rtogs pa ste //brtan pa de
180b3(1): dag rgyal ba'i sku skyes sras yin no //'du shes sgyu la mkhas pa ste //'gro ba thams cad 'du shes 'dra bar shes //smyig rgyu 'du shes 'dra ba ste //'gro ba thams can de ltar 'du shes phyind ci log //'du shes rnam
180b4(1): phyi mkhas pa ste //de ni 'gro kun 'du shes spangs pa yin //sems can sna tshogs khong du chud //'jig rten kun gyi sna tshogs rnam pa kun dang bral //'jig rten 'du shes rab tu shes //'jig rten 'du shes myed
180b5(1): myin 'du shes ldan //phyogs rnams 'du shes 'dra ba ste /sems can mtha' yas 'du shes rnam pa sna tshogs yin //ma nor myig dang ldan pa'i rnams //'jig rten sna tshogs 'du shes
180b6(1): rab tu mthong //'jig rten smyig rgyu ba ste //'du shes sna tshogs rnam pas 'jig rten rnam par bkod //'jig rten 'du shes gnas pa ste //de dag phyind ci log gsum
180b7(1): rnam par spangs //ji ltar smyig rgyu'i tshogs kyi rnams //'gro ba rnams la chu'i rnam pa dmyigs pas snang //de la chu ni yod myin yang //'chu'i 'du shes rnam par rab tu slu //
180b8(1): de bzhin 'jiga' rten thams cad kyang //khams gsum lam rgyud rnam pa sna tshogs mtha' yas te //'du shes rnam pa smyig rgyu bzhin //chags myed yid ldan spyod yul mtha' yas so //'du
180b9(1): 'du shes thams cad rnam par spangs //chags myed ye shes ldan zhing spros pa myed pa'i rnams //'gro ba'i 'du shes chags gyurd pa //'du shes bslus las brtan ba 'greld par byed //
180b10(1): nga rgyal rlom dang dregs pa spangs //'du shes thams cad bstsald cing 'jig rten 'drend pa byed //myi zad zad pa rtogs pa'i rnams //bden ba zad myi shes kyi tshul 'di yin //'jig rten 'di dag
180b11(1): thams cad rmyi lam lta bur khong du chud //chos rnams thams cad rmyi lam ngo bo nyid du rab tu rtogs //rmyi lam de dag yul la myi gnas phyogs la 'ang myi gnas te //rmyi lam lta bur 'jig rten rab tu zhi la de dag gzhol //
201a1(1): /pa dang /grol zhing bcings pa myed pa'i sems su kun tu bzang po'i rgyan kyi pha rold tu phyind pa thob ste /dmyigs pa re re zhing thams cad mkhyend pa nyid thams cad la zhugs kyang /thams cad 
201a2(1): mkhyend pa'i ye shes mthar thug par myi 'gyur ba'i tshul gyi rgyal po de lta bu thob par gyurd cig //dge ba'i rtsa ba 'dis chags pa myed pa dang /grol zhing bcing pa myed pa'i sems su /tshe 'di nas nye bar bzung ste /phyi 
201a3(1): ma'i mtha'i bskald pa'i mur thug par rmongs pa myed cing /rgyun myi 'chad par chos la gzhol zhing /byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i /spyod par gyurd pa'i rgyud brjod du myed pa'i /thams cad mkhyend pa nyid du /
201a4(1): rnam par 'tshang ba'i tshul thob par gyurd cig //dge ba'i rtsa ba 'dis chagasapa myed pa dang /grol zhi bcings pa myed pa'i semasasu byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa thams cad la /rab tu snang bar gyurd pa'i sems
201a5(1): bsgribs pa myed pa'i chos kyi snang ba rab tu 'thob pas /rgyun myi 'chad pa'i las la rab tu brtson zhing /byang chub sems pa'i kun tu bzang po'i rnam par 'phrul ba rab tu thob par gyurd cig /dge ba'i rtsa ba
201a6(1): 'dis chags pa myed pa dang/grol zhing bcings pa myed pa'i sems su byang chub sems dpa' kunatu bzang po'i spyod pa 'thob par bya ba la brtson zhing /thabs kyi tshul dang /mtha' yas pa'i tshul dang /
201a7(1): bsam gyis myi khyab pa'i tshul dang /byang chub sems dpa'i tshul dang /chos rnam pa tha dad pa bstand pa'i tshul dang /byang chubasems dpa'i 'dul ba thams cad kyi tshul dang /chos kyi 'khor lo mtha' dang dbung myed pa rab
201a8(1): tu bskord pa'i tshul dang /dus brjod du myed pa'i tshul dang /chos rnams pa tha dad pa bstand pa'i+i tshul dang /'jigs pa myed pa'i mdzod sa 'tshams myed pa'i tshul dang /grol zhing bcings pa skyed pa'i sems su phyir myi ldog pa'i sems can byang chub du yang
201a9(1): dag par 'byung ba nyams pa myed par bya ba dang /brtan ba dam pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba 'dis /sems can thams cad rnam par dag pa'i mthar phyind par byed cing /kun tu bzang po'i las kyis bsral bas 'gro ba thams cad dag par byed pa'i
201a10(1): lus kyi las yongs su rdzogs par gyurd cig //dge ba'i 'dis chags pa myed pa dang /grol zhing bcing pa myed pa'i sems su sems can gyi khams thams cad dga' bar bya ba'i tshig brjod du myed pa la rab tu brtson zhing /
201b1(1): ngag gi las kyi rgyan thams cad rnam par dag pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i bden kyi rlabs la brtson bas /sems can thams cad kyi rnam par brjod pa yongs su shes par bya ba'i /ye shes rnam par dag pa yongs su
201b2(1): thob par gyurd cig /dge ba'i rtsa ba 'dis kun tu bzang po'i spyod yul rab tu rtogs shing /chags pa myed pa dang grol zhing bcings pa myed pa'i sems su 'gro ba 'byam klas pa dang /zhing 'byam klas pa thams cad dang /sems can 'byam
201b3(1): klas pa thams cad dang /mngon bar shes pa dang /ye shes brjod du myed pa'i yang brjod du myed pa'i chos 'byam klas pa dang /chos 'byam klas pa'i rgyan de bzhin gshegs pa'i dkyil 'khord kyi mdzod thams cad nas byung ba dang /
201b4(1): lhag pa'i bsam bas rnam par dag pa'i sems kyi rgyud mnyam ba nyid kyis zhugs pa'i ye shes 'byam klas pa /rab tu rtogs par gyurd cig //dge ba'i rtsa ba 'dis chags pa myed pa dang /grol zhing bcings pa myed pa'
201b5(1): sems su /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi sku las byung ba'i lus rnam par dag pa dang /lhag pa'i bsam ba rnam par dag pas mos pa rab tu grub pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi byin kyi rlabs la brtson ba dang 
201b6(1): sangs rgyas kyi gzi brjid kyis yongs su bzung ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi yul yang dag par gnas pa dang /ye shes kyi rgya mtha' yas pas snang bar byas pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i lam gyis
201b7(1): mngon bar byung ba dang /tshig rab tu dbye ba thams cad la mkhas pa rnam par dag pa /khong du chud par bya ba la rab tu zhugs pa dang /theg pa chen po mtha' yas pa'i rnam par 'phrul bas rab tu byung
201b8(1): ba dang /'gro ba mtha' dang dbung myed pa thams cad /mngon bar rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar bya ba yang dag par bstand pa dang /byang chub semasadapa' kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa dang /smon lam dang yongs su
201b9(1): rdzogs pa thob par gyurd cig //dge ba'i rtsa ba 'dis mnyam ba nyid kyi smon lam gyi dbang pos brtson zhing /chags pa myed pa dang /grol zhing bcings pa myed pa'i sems su /dbang po rno ba dang /dbang po dge

282b1(1): pa byed //zhing dang bskald pa chos grangs sna tshogs du ma dang //mos dang bsam ba sems dang spyod pa sna tshogs dang //theg pa gsum po bstand pa tshad myed yang dag 'jug //bdag gi sems can de dag yongs su sbyin bya zhes //

282b4(1): rdul myed mkhas pa'i shes rab mchog //da dung mngon 'dod sngo ba de'i smon lam ste //myi thub stobs yin shind tu rtogs pa ye shes so //de ltar byang chub yon tan thams cad skad cig ldan //dang por dmyigs pas yon

288b7(1): dag 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' ye shes kyi sa brgyad pa ste /myi 'phrogs pa'i phyir myi g.yo ba zhes bya'o /ye shes myi zlogs pa'i phyir myi 'gyur ba zhes bya'o //'gro ba thams cad kyis shes par
